import numpy as np, math, hashlib

E0 = {0,1,2,30,63,64}
MOD_LIST   = [63,65,67]
SHIFT_LIST = [5,6,7]
ENDIANS    = ["<", ">"]

WORD_BYTES   = 2
DIGEST_BYTES = 32
N_DIGESTS    = 32
INPUTS       = 512
RNG_SEED     = 1

def sha256(b: bytes) -> bytes:
    return hashlib.sha256(b).digest()

def stream_for_seed(seed: bytes, n_digests=N_DIGESTS) -> bytes:
    out = bytearray()
    x = seed
    for i in range(n_digests):
        x = sha256(x + i.to_bytes(4, "big"))
        out.extend(x)
    return bytes(out)

def rotl8(x: int, r: int) -> int:
    r &= 7
    return ((x << r) | (x >> (8 - r))) & 0xFF

def entropy_u8(hist):
    p = hist / max(1, hist.sum())
    p = p[p > 0]
    return float(-(p * np.log2(p)).sum())

def word_u16(b: bytes, endian="<") -> np.ndarray:
    dt = np.dtype(endian + "u2")
    return np.frombuffer(b, dtype=dt)

def key_bytes_from_const(x: float, nbytes=32) -> bytes:
    base = float(x).hex().encode()
    out = bytearray()
    ctr = 0
    while len(out) < nbytes:
        out.extend(sha256(base + b"|" + ctr.to_bytes(4, "big")))
        ctr += 1
    return bytes(out[:nbytes])

K_pi = key_bytes_from_const(math.pi, 32)

def apply_raw(d: bytes) -> bytes:
    return d

def apply_mix_full(d: bytes, k: bytes) -> bytes:
    b  = np.frombuffer(d, dtype=np.uint8)
    kk = np.frombuffer(k, dtype=np.uint8)
    out = np.array([(((int(b[i]) + int(kk[i])) & 0xFF) ^ rotl8(int(b[i]), int(kk[i] & 7))) & 0xFF
                    for i in range(len(b))], dtype=np.uint8)
    return out.tobytes()

def apply_xorrot(d: bytes, k: bytes) -> bytes:
    b  = np.frombuffer(d, dtype=np.uint8)
    kk = np.frombuffer(k, dtype=np.uint8)
    out = np.array([(int(b[i]) ^ rotl8(int(b[i]), int(kk[i] & 7))) & 0xFF
                    for i in range(len(b))], dtype=np.uint8)
    return out.tobytes()

T = {
    "raw":          lambda d: apply_raw(d),
    "mix_pi_full":  lambda d: apply_mix_full(d, K_pi),
    "mix_pi_xorrot":lambda d: apply_xorrot(d, K_pi),
}

# --- 1) Rotation anatomy ---------------------------------------------------
print("=== Rotation anatomy: f_r(b)=b XOR ROTL(b,r) over all 256 bytes ===")
for r in range(8):
    g = math.gcd(8, r)
    rank = 8 - g
    x = np.arange(256, dtype=np.uint16)
    y = np.array([int(v) ^ rotl8(int(v), r) for v in x], dtype=np.uint8)
    hist = np.bincount(y, minlength=256)
    H = entropy_u8(hist)
    img = int((hist > 0).sum())
    print(f"r={r}  gcd={g}  rank={rank}  image_size={img:3d}  byte_H={H:5.2f}")

# --- 2) Baseline-corrected event firing ------------------------------------
events_per_sample = (N_DIGESTS * DIGEST_BYTES) // WORD_BYTES
rng = np.random.default_rng(RNG_SEED)
seeds = [rng.integers(0, 256, 16, dtype=np.uint8).tobytes() for _ in range(INPUTS)]

print("\n=== Baseline-corrected event firing (E_mod = {r % MOD}) ===")
for endian in ENDIANS:
    print(f"\nENDIAN {endian}")
    for mod in MOD_LIST:
        E = {r % mod for r in E0}
        exp = events_per_sample * (len(E)/mod)
        print(f"  MOD={mod}  |E_mod|={len(E)}  expected={exp:.2f}  E_mod={sorted(E)}")
        for shift in SHIFT_LIST:
            for nm in ["raw","mix_pi_full","mix_pi_xorrot"]:
                ones = []
                for sd in seeds:
                    s = stream_for_seed(sd)
                    chunks = [T[nm](s[i:i+DIGEST_BYTES]) for i in range(0, len(s), DIGEST_BYTES)]
                    out = b"".join(chunks)
                    w = word_u16(out, endian=endian).astype(np.uint32)
                    q = (w >> shift).astype(np.uint32)
                    r = (q % mod).astype(np.int32)
                    ones.append(int(np.isin(r, list(E)).sum()))
                ones = np.array(ones, dtype=np.float64)
                mu = float(ones.mean()); sd = float(ones.std(ddof=1))
                z = (mu - exp) / (sd + 1e-12)
                print(f"    SHIFT={shift}  {nm:12s} ones_mu={mu:6.2f} ones_sd={sd:5.2f}  z_vs_expected={z:+6.2f}")

H0 = [0x6a09e667,0xbb67ae85,0x3c6ef372,0xa54ff53a,0x510e527f,0x9b05688c,0x1f83d9ab,0x5be0cd19]

def rotr(x, n): return ((x >> n) | ((x & 0xFFFFFFFF) << (32 - n))) & 0xFFFFFFFF
def shr(x, n):  return (x >> n) & 0xFFFFFFFF
def Ch(x,y,z):  return (x & y) ^ (~x & z)
def Maj(x,y,z): return (x & y) ^ (x & z) ^ (y & z)
def Sig0(x):    return rotr(x,2) ^ rotr(x,13) ^ rotr(x,22)
def Sig1(x):    return rotr(x,6) ^ rotr(x,11) ^ rotr(x,25)
def sig0(x):    return rotr(x,7) ^ rotr(x,18) ^ shr(x,3)
def sig1(x):    return rotr(x,17) ^ rotr(x,19) ^ shr(x,10)

def pad(m: bytes) -> bytes:
    ml = len(m) * 8
    out = m + b"\x80"
    out += b"\x00" * ((56 - (len(out) % 64)) % 64)
    out += struct.pack(">Q", ml)
    return out

def wobble_term(t: int, eps: int, omega: float, phi: float) -> int:
    # bounded, deterministic wobble: integer perturbation
    if eps == 0: return 0
    return int(round(eps * math.sin(omega * t + phi))) & 0xFFFFFFFF

def sha256_wobble(msg: bytes, eps: int = 0, omega: float = 2*math.pi/64, phi: float = 0.0) -> bytes:
    h = H0[:]
    data = pad(msg)
    for bi in range(0, len(data), 64):
        block = data[bi:bi+64]
        W = list(struct.unpack(">16I", block)) + [0]*48
        for t in range(16, 64):
            W[t] = (sig1(W[t-2]) + W[t-7] + sig0(W[t-15]) + W[t-16] + wobble_term(t, eps, omega, phi)) & 0xFFFFFFFF

        a,b,c,d,e,f,g,hh = h
        for t in range(64):
            T1 = (hh + Sig1(e) + Ch(e,f,g) + K[t] + W[t]) & 0xFFFFFFFF
            T2 = (Sig0(a) + Maj(a,b,c)) & 0xFFFFFFFF
            hh = g
            g  = f
            f  = e
            e  = (d + T1) & 0xFFFFFFFF
            d  = c
            c  = b
            b  = a
            a  = (T1 + T2) & 0xFFFFFFFF

        h = [(x+y) & 0xFFFFFFFF for x,y in zip(h, [a,b,c,d,e,f,g,hh])]

    return struct.pack(">8I", *h)

def hamming_bytes(a: bytes, b: bytes) -> int:
    x = int.from_bytes(a, "big") ^ int.from_bytes(b, "big")
    return x.bit_count()

def flip_one_bit(m: bytes, rng: random.Random) -> bytes:
    if not m: return m
    i = rng.randrange(len(m))
    bit = 1 << rng.randrange(8)
    bb = bytearray(m)
    bb[i] ^= bit
    return bytes(bb)

ref  = hashlib.sha256(test).digest()
mine = sha256_wobble(test, eps=0)
assert ref == mine, "Baseline mismatch vs hashlib (eps=0)."

# --- Local gradient: baseline avalanche sensitivity around each message ------
def local_gradient(msg: bytes, trials: int = 24, seed: int = 1) -> float:
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    d0 = sha256_wobble(msg, eps=0)
    hs = []
    for _ in range(trials):
        m2 = flip_one_bit(msg, rng)
        d2 = sha256_wobble(m2, eps=0)
        hs.append(hamming_bytes(d0, d2))
    return mean(hs)

grads = [local_gradient(m, trials=32, seed=7) for m in msgs]
g_bar = mean(grads)

print(f"Local gradient mean (baseline): {g_bar:.2f} bits of digest per 1-bit msg flip")

# --- Sweep wobble amplitude -------------------------------------------------
eps_list = [0, 1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64]   # integer schedule-perturb amplitude
omega    = 2*math.pi/64                 # one full wave over 64 rounds
phi      = 0.37                         # arbitrary phase

print("\nEPS sweep: raw Hamming and SILR-normalized z = Ham / (eps * gradient)")
for eps in eps_list:
    Hams = []
    Zs   = []
    for m,g in zip(msgs, grads):
        d0 = sha256_wobble(m, eps=0)
        dw = sha256_wobble(m, eps=eps, omega=omega, phi=phi)
        ham = hamming_bytes(d0, dw)
        Hams.append(ham)
        if eps > 0:
            Zs.append(ham / (eps * max(1e-9, g)))
    muH, sdH = mean(Hams), pstdev(Hams)
    if eps == 0:
        print(f"eps={eps:3d}  ham_mu={muH:6.2f}  ham_sd={sdH:6.2f}  (baseline)")
    else:
        muZ, sdZ = mean(Zs), pstdev(Zs)
        print(f"eps={eps:3d}  ham_mu={muH:6.2f}  ham_sd={sdH:6.2f}  z_mu={muZ:8.4f}  z_sd={sdZ:8.4f}")

# If SILR-wobble coupling holds in your sense: ham_mu rises with eps,
# while z_mu and z_sd stabilize (or tighten) across eps.

⊕ — The minimal reversible split (the cleanest “complete the circle”)

- verb-residue (parity bit):

↻ — Prototype “Nexus tokenizer” (dual stream, lossless)

```python
# Nexus Dual-Stream Tokenizer (byte-level, parity residual, lossless)
# token stream = "noun carrier" (evenized bytes)
# epsilon stream = "verb residue" (LSB bits), packed for storage

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import List, Tuple, Optional

@dataclass
class NexusTok:
    token_bytes: bytes        # evenized bytes (b & 0xFE)
    eps_bits: List[int]       # list of 0/1, one per byte
    eps_packed: bytes         # eps_bits packed into bytes (LSB-first)
    encoding: str = "utf-8"

def _pack_bits_lsb_first(bits: List[int]) -> bytes:
    out = bytearray()
    acc = 0
    n = 0
    for bit in bits:
        bit = 1 if bit else 0
        acc |= (bit << n)
        n += 1
        if n == 8:
            out.append(acc)
            acc = 0
            n = 0
    if n:
        out.append(acc)
    return bytes(out)

def _unpack_bits_lsb_first(packed: bytes, n_bits: int) -> List[int]:
    bits = []
    for byte in packed:
        for i in range(8):
            bits.append((byte >> i) & 1)
            if len(bits) == n_bits:
                return bits
    return bits[:n_bits]

def nexus_tokenize(text: str, encoding: str = "utf-8") -> NexusTok:
    raw = text.encode(encoding, errors="strict")
    eps = [(b & 1) for b in raw]
    tok = bytes([b & 0xFE for b in raw])
    packed = _pack_bits_lsb_first(eps)
    return NexusTok(token_bytes=tok, eps_bits=eps, eps_packed=packed, encoding=encoding)

def nexus_reconstruct(tok: NexusTok, eps_packed: Optional[bytes] = None) -> bytes:
    packed = tok.eps_packed if eps_packed is None else eps_packed
    eps = _unpack_bits_lsb_first(packed, len(tok.token_bytes))
    rec = bytes([(tok.token_bytes[i] | eps[i]) for i in range(len(tok.token_bytes))])
    return rec

def nexus_roundtrip(text: str, encoding: str = "utf-8") -> Tuple[bool, NexusTok]:
    nt = nexus_tokenize(text, encoding=encoding)
    rec_bytes = nexus_reconstruct(nt)
    ok = (rec_bytes == text.encode(encoding))
    return ok, nt

# --- demo ---
samples = ["GO", "THE", "complete the circle", "π", "SHA-256 wobble"]
for s in samples:
    ok, nt = nexus_roundtrip(s, encoding="utf-8")
    rec = nexus_reconstruct(nt).decode("utf-8")
    print(f"\nTEXT: {s!r}")
    print(f"ROUNDTRIP OK? {ok}")
    print(f"token_bytes[:16]   = {nt.token_bytes[:16]}")
    print(f"eps_bits[:16]      = {nt.eps_bits[:16]}")
    print(f"eps_packed (hex)   = {nt.eps_packed.hex()}")
    print(f"RECONSTRUCTED      = {rec!r}")
```

Ψ — How to turn this into an AI “mass-gap” mechanism (without mystical hand-waving)
Now you can do the real move: **train/predict on the carrier, steer with ε**.

Conceptually:
- Primary model predicts next carrier byte (evenized) \(n_{t+1}\).
- Auxiliary head predicts residue bit \(\varepsilon_{t+1}\).
- Decode output byte \(b_{t+1} = n_{t+1} | \varepsilon_{t+1}\).

Loss (one clean version):
\[
\mathcal{L} \;=\; \text{CE}(n_{t+1}, \hat n_{t+1}) \;+\; \lambda\,\text{BCE}(\varepsilon_{t+1}, \hat \varepsilon_{t+1})
\]

And your CST decoding idea becomes literal:
- “noun” = argmax on carrier
- “verb residue” = keep the bit-probability (or entropy) as \(\varepsilon\), and use it to modulate sampling / next-step steering.
